Missing material: material.ridgeLumber: Using generic 2 × 6 framing lumber dimensions for geometry only. Exact product, grade/species, exposure and installation suitability are unresolved.
Unresolved: greenhouse-loads: Site wind/snow loads, framing grade/species, spans, bracing and foundation/anchors are not qualified.
Unresolved: roof-bearing: Rafters have real plumb-cut geometry, but birdsmouths, rafter hangers/bearings, tie-downs and their installation schedules are not generated or approved.
Unresolved: greenhouse-glazing-system: Source-matched SUNLITE multiwall sheet envelopes and real gable/roof cuts are represented, with flutes along vertical or roof-slope lengths. Thermal gaps, edge/profile engagement, horizontal eave/gable seams, UV-side marking, tapes, drainage, expansion drilling and actual fastening/support schedules are unresolved; shown cut blanks have no system allowance deductions.
Unresolved: greenhouse-ventilation: An opening roof vent, its framed opening, hardware, vent sizing/control and condensation plan remain unrepresented.
Unresolved: door-hardware: The closed/open door frame is a dimensional pivot preview. Actual hinges, latch, stops, glazing and mounting hardware are unspecified and unpriced.
Unresolved: material.greenhouseGlazing.installation: 132506 8 mm SUNLITE multiwall greenhouse glazing only; source-listed material application, not approval of a generated roof or enclosure. Use the SUNLITE 8 mm multiwall accessory family; do not substitute corrugated sheets, metal roofing or PVC glazing by matching overall dimensions. Resolve support spacing, site loads, roof slope, thermal movement and fastening from the selected panel installation instructions. Orient the UV-protected face and flutes correctly; provide appropriate top/bottom tapes, U/H profiles, drainage and ridge/edge details.
Unresolved: greenhouse-envelope-profile: Sheets are represented as physical 8 mm outer envelopes, not internal cell ribs. No end-to-end flute splice, panel load capacity or weather-tight installation is approved. Oversized downslope blanks require different stock/geometry, not an invented H-profile end joint.
